      Control of Corruption
      About this indicator
      This indicator shows the World Bank’s Control of Corruption score, an estimate of how far public power in a country is used for private gain, including petty and grand corruption and capture of the state by elites and private interests. It combines perceptions data from many sources. Scores run from 0 to 100, and higher scores mean stronger control of corruption. Scores are rounded to whole numbers.
      Official sources
      
        Worldwide Governance Indicatorsworldbank.org · the World Bank’s governance data, including the Control of Corruption scores shown here
        Corruption Perceptions Index 2025transparency.org · Transparency International’s separate corruption index, for comparison
      
      Data from World Bank, Worldwide Governance Indicators, Control of Corruption governance score (0 to 100), 2025 data, accessed 24 November 2026, licensed under CC BY 4.0. This is an adaptation of an original work by The World Bank. Views and opinions expressed in the adaptation are the sole responsibility of the author or authors of the adaptation and are not endorsed by The World Bank.
    
    
      Financial Action Task Force (FATF) status
      About this indicator
      This indicator shows whether the Financial Action Task Force (FATF), the intergovernmental body that sets global standards against money laundering and terrorist financing, has placed a country on one of its two lists for weaknesses in those defenses. The black list is FATF’s list of High-Risk Jurisdictions subject to a Call for Action. The grey list is its list of Jurisdictions under Increased Monitoring. Every other jurisdiction is shown as not listed.
      The country details box also shows when each country was last evaluated by FATF or its regional body, with a link to the published ratings on FATF’s website.
      Official sources
      
        Black and grey listsfatf-gafi.org · FATF’s current lists of high-risk and monitored jurisdictions
        Consolidated assessment ratingsfatf-gafi.org · mutual evaluation ratings for every assessed jurisdiction, with links to each report
      
      Data from FATF (October 2026), High-Risk Jurisdictions subject to a Call for Action and Jurisdictions under Increased Monitoring, licensed under CC BY 4.0, as of the FATF Plenary of 28 to 30 October 2026; FATF (2026), Consolidated assessment ratings, 2013 and 2022 Methodologies (accessed on 24 October 2026), for evaluation dates and assessment bodies.
      This is an adaptation of an original work by the Financial Action Task Force (FATF). The opinions expressed and arguments employed in this adaptation should not be reported as representing the official views of the FATF or of its Members or of its Secretariat.
    
    
      Foreign Terrorist Organization (FTO) presence
      About this indicator
      This indicator summarizes Paragon’s reading of what the U.S. State Department reports about designated Foreign Terrorist Organizations in each country. Paragon does not independently assess whether a group is present. Groups are listed by their official names. “Identified” means the State Department’s Country Reports on Terrorism 2024, or its designation statement for a group designated since, describes the group as active in the country (for example, carrying out repeated attacks or controlling territory), based there, or raising money or recruiting there. Not counted: arrests of individuals, a single or inspired attack, threats, transit, the country’s nationals fighting abroad, activity across a border, and passing mentions. “None identified” means Paragon found no such presence in the sources it reviewed; it is not a guarantee that none exists. “Not covered” means the State Department’s report has no chapter on the country; it does not mean no designated group is present. Other U.S. terrorism listings and other governments’ lists are not included.A country-level result does not show where in the country a group is active. The State Department’s report may name the regions, so check it against where the grantee works.
      Official sources
      
        Foreign Terrorist Organizationsstate.gov · the U.S. State Department’s current list of designated organizations
        Country Reports on Terrorism 2024state.gov · the State Department’s country-by-country reporting used to identify presence
      
      Country presence reflects Paragon Philanthropy’s reading of the State Department’s Country Reports on Terrorism 2024 and subsequent designation notices, current through 24 November 2026. Countries outside that reading are shown as not covered.
      U.S. government information is summarized by Paragon Philanthropy from the sources named.
    
    
      Sanctions
      About this indicator
      This indicator shows the sanctions programs that the United States, the European Union, the United Nations, the United Kingdom, Canada, Switzerland and Australia maintain for a specific country, whether they restrict dealings with the country as a whole or target particular people, entities or sectors there. U.S. programs are labeled “comprehensive” where they embargo nearly all dealings with the country, and “targeted” otherwise. Sanctions that are not tied to a country, such as counter-terrorism, narcotics, cyber and human rights sanctions, can apply to listed persons anywhere and are not shown. The EU also applies every UN sanctions regime.
      
      Official sources
      
        Sanctions Programs and Country Informationofac.treasury.gov · U.S. Treasury (OFAC) country programs, with each program’s rules and guidance
        EU Sanctions Mapsanctionsmap.eu · EU restrictive measures by country
        UN Security Council sanctions regimesun.org · each UN sanctions regime and its committee
        UK sanctions regimesgov.uk · each UK regime and its guidance
        Current sanctions imposed by Canadainternational.gc.ca · Global Affairs Canada country sanctions, with links to each regulation
        Swiss sanctions ordinances (SECO)seco.admin.ch · the ordinances in force under the Embargo Act, in German, French and Italian
        Australian sanctions (DFAT)dfat.gov.au · the Australian Sanctions Office’s sanctions frameworks, UN and autonomous
      
      Data from U.S. Department of the Treasury, Office of Foreign Assets Control (OFAC); European Union; United Nations Security Council; UK Foreign, Commonwealth and Development Office; Global Affairs Canada and the Justice Laws website; Swiss State Secretariat for Economic Affairs (SECO) and Fedlex; Australian Department of Foreign Affairs and Trade (DFAT). Checked 24 November 2026. Program and regime names are summarized by Paragon Philanthropy from each source; regime titles link to the official texts, and nothing here is an official version. EU information © European Union, reused under CC BY 4.0. UK information contains public sector information licensed under the Open Government Licence v3.0. Swiss ordinance titles are Paragon Philanthropy’s English renderings of the official German titles; each links to the official text on Fedlex. Australian information: Department of Foreign Affairs and Trade website, www.dfat.gov.au, licensed under CC BY 4.0; some DFAT framework pages are reached through the Australian Sanctions Office Annual Report 2025.
    
    
      How Paragon Can Help
      Country indicators, such as sanctions programs, FATF listing, terrorist group presence and corruption scores, describe the country where a grantee operates. They say nothing about the grantee itself. Paragon’s vetting team screens what country indicators do not cover: the organization, the people who run it, and the entities and banks around it, at the level of review you set for each grant.
      1Anti-Terrorist Financing Screening (ATFS) screens the organization, its leadership, its owners and controlling entities, and its banks against sanctions and terrorism lists. Its report records the country indicators on this map as escalation considerations.
      2Higher-Risk Anti-Terrorist Financing Screening (ATFS-HR) adds national sanctions screening, adverse media review, banking documentation, and review of policies and downstream partner oversight, for grants where several risk factors coincide.
      Politically Exposed Persons Screening checks the same individuals against records of current and former public officials and their relatives and close associates.
      Further reading
      The When to Escalate guide explains which country and grant-level indicators may call for review beyond a baseline screen.
      The Four-Tier Framework sets out four levels of review, from baseline screening to integrity due diligence, matched to the risk of each grant.
      Risk Factors in International Grantmaking covers the grant-level factors that sit alongside these country indicators, such as grant size, regranting and first-time relationships.

No map colour changed.

const stops = [[0, [246, 192, 92]], [25, [228, 160, 68]], [45, [182, 126, 62]], [65, [132, 100, 68]], [90, [94, 80, 66]]];
function cpiColor(s) {
  for (let i = 1; i < stops.length; i++) if (s <= stops[i][0]) {
    const [a, ca] = stops[i - 1], [b, cb] = stops[i], k = (s - a) / (b - a);
    return "rgb(" + ca.map((v, j) => Math.round(v + (cb[j] - v) * k)).join(",") + ")";
  }
  return "rgb(" + stops[stops.length - 1][1].join(",") + ")";
}

function fmeDate(s) { const p = s.split("-"); return FME_MON[+p[1] - 1] + " " + p[0]; }
function fme(key, nm) {
  const f = FME[key];
  const def = `<p class="fme-def">A mutual evaluation is a peer review in which FATF or a regional body rates how well a country's defenses against money laundering and terrorist financing work in practice.</p>`;
  if (!f) return `<div class="fme"><p class="fme-h">Latest FATF evaluation of ${nm}</p>${def}<p>No published FATF evaluation for ${nm}.</p></div>`;
  const [mer, upd, body] = f;
  const now = new Date(); const old = (now.getFullYear() * 12 + now.getMonth()) - (+mer.slice(0, 4) * 12 + +mer.slice(5, 7) - 1) >= 60 ? " This evaluation is more than five years old." : "";
  return `<div class="fme"><p class="fme-h">Latest FATF evaluation of ${nm}</p>${def}<p><b>${nm}</b> was last evaluated by ${body} in ${fmeDate(mer)}.${old}</p><p class="fme-n"><a href="https://www.fatf-gafi.org/en/publications/mutualevaluations/documents/assessment-ratings.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">See ${nm}'s published ratings on fatf-gafi.org</a></p></div>`;
}

  black: "FATF finds significant strategic deficiencies in this jurisdiction's systems against money laundering and terrorist financing, and calls on its members to apply enhanced due diligence and, in the most serious cases, countermeasures.",

const nameOf = k => (RISK[k] && RISK[k].n) || k;

function colorFor(d, m) {
  d = d || {};
  if (isStale(m)) return NODATA;
  if (m === "cpi") return d.cpi != null ? cpiColor(d.cpi) : NODATA;
  if (m === "fto") return d.fto === "y" ? css("--fto") : d.fto === "n" ? FTO_NONE : NODATA;
  if (m === "sanc") { if (d.us === "c") return css("--embargo"); const n = bodies(d); return n >= 5 ? css("--s3") : n >= 3 ? css("--s2") : n ? css("--s1") : PLAIN; }
  if (d.fatf === "black") return css("--call");
  if (d.fatf === "grey") return css("--grey-list");
  return PLAIN;
}
const fill = f => colorFor(info(f), mode);
const liftCtx = document.createElement("canvas").getContext("2d");
const lift = col => { liftCtx.fillStyle = col; const h = liftCtx.fillStyle.replace("#", ""); if (h.length !== 6) return col; return "rgb(" + [0, 2, 4].map(i => Math.round(parseInt(h.slice(i, i + 2), 16) * 0.65 + 255 * 0.35)).join(",") + ")"; };
const dim = col => { liftCtx.fillStyle = col; const v = liftCtx.fillStyle; let r = v[0] === "#" ? [0, 2, 4].map(i => parseInt(v.slice(1 + i, 3 + i), 16)).concat([1]) : v.replace("rgba(", "").replace("rgb(", "").replace(")", "").split(",").map(Number); if (r.length === 3) r.push(1); return "rgba(" + r.slice(0, 3).map(c => Math.round(c * 0.55 + 20 * 0.45)).join(",") + "," + r[3] + ")"; }; /* dim the rest of the globe when a country is selected 2026-10-26 */
const capFill = f => f === selected ? lift(fill(f)) : selected ? dim(fill(f)) : fill(f);
function hasStatus(d, m) {
  d = d || {};
  if (m === "cpi") return d.cpi != null;
  if (m === "fatf") return !!d.fatf;
  if (m === "fto") return d.fto === "y";
  return bodies(d) > 0;
}
function shortText(d, m) {
  d = d || {};
  if (isStale(m)) return "Being updated";
  if (m === "cpi") return d.cpi != null ? `${d.cpi} / 100` : "Not scored";
  if (m === "fatf") return d.fatf ? FATF_SHORT[d.fatf] : "Not listed";
  if (m === "fto") return d.fto === "y" ? "Identified" : d.fto === "n" ? "None identified" : "Not covered";
  const nb = bodies(d); return d.us === "c" ? "U.S. comprehensive" + (nb > 1 ? " + " + (nb - 1) + " more" : "") : nb ? nb + " of 7 jurisdictions" : "None";
}
const ord = k => { const s = ["th", "st", "nd", "rd"], v = k % 100; return k + (s[(v - 20) % 10] || s[v] || s[0]); };
function tip(f) {
  const d = info(f) || {}, name = d.n || f.properties.name;
  let body;
  if (isStale(mode)) body = "This view is being updated.";
  else if (mode === "cpi") body = d.cpi != null ? `Control of Corruption: <b>${d.cpi}</b> / 100<br><span class="tipmore">Higher is better · World Bank</span>` : "Not scored by the World Bank";
  else if (mode === "fatf") body = d.fatf ? `<b>${FATF_SHORT[d.fatf]}</b><br>${FATF_LABEL[d.fatf]}` : "Not on the FATF black or grey list";
  else if (mode === "fto") body = d.fto === "y" ? `U.S.-designated FTOs present: <b>${d.ftog.length}</b><ul class="tipl">` + d.ftog.slice(0, 3).map(g => `<li>${g}</li>`).join("") + "</ul>" + (d.ftog.length > 3 ? `<span class="tipmore">and ${d.ftog.length - 3} more. Select the country for all.</span>` : "") : FTO_SHORT[d.fto || "a"];
  else { const oth = JUR.slice(1).filter(j => d[j[0]]).map(j => j[2]); body = `U.S.: ${d.us === "c" ? "comprehensive" : d.us === "t" ? "targeted" : "none"}<br>Others in force: ${oth.length ? oth.join(", ") : "none"}`; }
  return `<div class="tip"><div class="tipn">${name}</div>${body}</div>`;
}

// Small countries get a dot so they stay visible at globe scale.
function ringBounds(f) {
  let best = [], g = f.geometry;
  const rings = g.type === "Polygon" ? [g.coordinates[0]] : g.coordinates.map(p => p[0]);
  rings.forEach(r => { if (r.length > best.length) best = r; });
  const xs = best.map(p => p[0]), ys = best.map(p => p[1]);
  return [[Math.min(...xs), Math.min(...ys)], [Math.max(...xs), Math.max(...ys)]];
}
const SMALL = feats.map(f => { const [[x0, y0], [x1, y1]] = ringBounds(f); return { f, lat: (y0 + y1) / 2, lng: (x0 + x1) / 2, area: (x1 - x0) * (y1 - y0) }; })
  .filter(s => 3 > s.area && RISK[s.f.properties.name]);
const dots = () => isStale(mode) ? [] : SMALL.filter(s => hasStatus(info(s.f), mode));

const reduce = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;
const touch = window.matchMedia("(pointer: coarse)").matches;
const container = el("globe");
const world = Globe()(container)
  .backgroundColor("rgba(0,0,0,0)")
  .showAtmosphere(true).atmosphereColor("#7fa6c9").atmosphereAltitude(0.17).showGraticules(true)
  .polygonsData(feats)
  .polygonAltitude(f => f === selected ? 0.07 : f === hovered ? 0.02 : 0.006)
  .polygonCapColor(capFill)
  .polygonSideColor(f => f === selected ? "rgba(233,160,102,0.9)" : "rgba(0,0,0,0.45)")
  .polygonStrokeColor(f => f === selected ? "#ffffff" : "rgba(255,244,230,0.22)")
  .polygonLabel(tip)
  .onPolygonHover(f => { hovered = f; world.polygonAltitude(world.polygonAltitude()); container.style.cursor = f ? "pointer" : "grab"; })
  .onPolygonClick(f => select(f))
  .polygonsTransitionDuration(220)
  .pointsData(dots())
  .pointLat("lat").pointLng("lng").pointAltitude(0.012).pointRadius(0.55)
  .pointColor(s => selected ? (s.f === selected ? lift(colorFor(info(s.f), mode)) : dim(colorFor(info(s.f), mode))) : colorFor(info(s.f), mode))
  .pointLabel(s => tip(s.f))

function select(f, fly) {
  selected = f || null;
  world.polygonAltitude(world.polygonAltitude()).polygonStrokeColor(world.polygonStrokeColor()).polygonSideColor(world.polygonSideColor()).polygonCapColor(capFill); world.pointColor(world.pointColor());
  ctl.autoRotate = false;
  if (f && fly) { const [[x0, y0], [x1, y1]] = ringBounds(f); const span = Math.max(x1 - x0, (y1 - y0) * 1.3); ctl.autoRotate = false; const alt = Math.min(1.5, Math.max(0.85, 0.5 + span * 0.02)); world.pointOfView({ lat: (y0 + y1) / 2 - 9 * alt, lng: (x0 + x1) / 2, altitude: alt }, 1100); }
  writeHash(); render();
}

function block(d, m) {
  const L = LAYERS[m];
  if (isStale(m)) return `<div class="metric"><div class="stale">This view is being updated. Please consult the source directly.</div></div>`;
  if (m === "cpi") {
    if (d.cpi == null) return `<div class="metric"><div class="label">Control of Corruption · World Bank</div><div class="sub">Not scored in the Worldwide Governance Indicators.</div></div>`;
    let chg = "";
    if (d.prev != null) { const c = d.cpi - d.prev; chg = c === 0 ? "Score unchanged since 2024." : `Score ${c > 0 ? "up" : "down"} ${Math.abs(c)} point${Math.abs(c) === 1 ? "" : "s"} since 2024.`; }
    return `<div class="metric"><div class="label">Control of Corruption · World Bank</div><div class="big">${d.cpi}<small> / 100</small></div><div class="sub">Score out of 100, where higher means stronger control of corruption. ${chg}</div></div>`;
  }
  if (m === "fatf") {
    if (!d.fatf) return `<div class="metric"><div class="label">FATF status</div><div class="status">Not listed</div><div class="sub">Not on the FATF black list or grey list.</div></div>`;
    const c = d.fatf === "black" ? css("--call") : css("--grey-list");
    return `<div class="metric"><div class="label">FATF status</div>
      <div class="badge" style="--b:${c}">${FATF_SHORT[d.fatf]}</div>
      <div class="status" style="font-size:16px">${FATF_LABEL[d.fatf]}</div>
      <div class="sub">${FATF_DESC[d.fatf]}</div></div>`;
  }
  if (m === "fto") {
    let b;
    if (!d.fto || d.fto === "a") b = `<div class="status">${sw(NODATA)}Not covered</div><div class="sub">The State Department&#8217;s Country Reports on Terrorism 2024 has no chapter on this country. <b>A designated group may still be present.</b></div>`;
    else if (d.fto === "n") b = `<div class="status">${sw(FTO_NONE)}None identified</div><div class="sub">Paragon has not identified a designated group active, based, or raising money or recruiting here in the State Department reporting it reviewed. <b>This is not a guarantee that none is present.</b></div>`;
    else b = `<div class="status">${sw(css("--fto"))}Identified (${d.ftog.length} ${d.ftog.length === 1 ? "group" : "groups"})</div><div class="sub">In the State Department's reporting, as read by Paragon.</div>`;
    return `<div class="metric"><div class="label">FTO presence</div>${b}</div>`;
  }
  const row = (lab, val) => `<div class="srow"><div class="slab">${lab}</div><div class="sval">${val}</div></div>`;
  const np = d.usp ? d.usp.length : 0;
  const usv = d.us ? (d.us === "c" ? sw(css("--embargo")) + "Comprehensive sanctions" : (np > 1 ? `Targeted programs (${np})` : "Targeted program")) : "No country program";
  const jv = k => d[k] ? (d[k].length > 1 ? `In force (${d[k].length})` : "In force") : "None";
  return `<div class="metric sanc"><div class="label">Sanctions</div>${row("United States", usv)}${JUR.slice(1).map(j => row(j[1], jv(j[0]))).join("")}</div>`;
}
const PP = (href, text) => `<a class="pp" href="${href}">${text}</a>`;
function signals(d) {
  const s = [];
  if (d.fatf === "black") s.push("a FATF black listing"); else if (d.fatf) s.push("a FATF grey listing");
  if (d.fto === "y") s.push((d.ftog ? d.ftog.length : 0) > 1 ? "designated terrorist groups present" : "a designated terrorist group present");
  if (d.us === "c") s.push("comprehensive U.S. sanctions"); else if (bodies(d)) s.push("a country sanctions program");
  return s;
}
const joinList = a => a.length > 1 ? a.slice(0, -1).join(", ") + " and " + a[a.length - 1] : a[0];
let STEP_ACT = 0;
function showStep(n) {
  STEP_ACT = n;
  document.querySelectorAll("#ppci .cta").forEach(c => {
    c.querySelectorAll(".steps li.st").forEach(li => { const on = +li.dataset.n === n; li.classList.toggle("open", on); li.querySelector(".st-h").setAttribute("aria-expanded", on); });
    c.querySelectorAll(".st-pane .st-b").forEach(bd => bd.classList.toggle("on", +bd.dataset.n === n));
    const p = c.querySelector(".st-pane"); if (p) p.classList.toggle("on", n > 0);
  });
  placePane();
}
function placePane() {
  document.querySelectorAll("#ppci .cta").forEach(c => {
    const p = c.querySelector(".st-pane");
    if (!p) return;
    p.classList.remove("left"); c.classList.remove("pane-left"); p.style.top = "";
    if (!p.classList.contains("on")) return;
    if (getComputedStyle(p).position !== "absolute") return;
    const cr = c.getBoundingClientRect(), h = p.offsetHeight;
    p.classList.add("left"); c.classList.add("pane-left");
    const ph = p.offsetHeight;
    let t = cr.height - ph;
    if (cr.top + t + ph > window.innerHeight - 12) t = window.innerHeight - 12 - ph - cr.top;
    if (cr.top + t < 12) t = 12 - cr.top;
    p.style.top = t + "px";
  });
}
window.addEventListener("scroll", () => { if (STEP_ACT > 0) placePane(); }, { passive: true });
window.addEventListener("resize", () => { if (STEP_ACT > 0) placePane(); });
function mountSteps() {
  const c = document.querySelector("#ppci #panel .cta");
  if (!c) return;
  const ol = c.querySelector(".steps");
  const pane = document.createElement("div");
  pane.className = "st-pane";
  ol.querySelectorAll("li.st").forEach(li => { const bd = li.querySelector(".st-b"); if (bd) { bd.dataset.n = li.dataset.n; pane.appendChild(bd); } });
  ol.after(pane);
  showStep(STEP_ACT);
}
document.addEventListener("click", e => {
  const h = e.target.closest ? e.target.closest("#ppci .st-h") : null;
  if (!h) return;
  const n = +h.closest("li").dataset.n;
  const hov = window.matchMedia("(hover: hover) and (min-width: 881px)").matches;
  showStep(hov ? n : (STEP_ACT === n ? 0 : n));
});

  const b = e.target.closest ? e.target.closest("#ppci .cn-link") : null;
  if (!b) return;
  const t = el("cdet-box");
  if (t) t.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
});
let STEP_T = null, STEP_OUT = null;
if (matchMedia("(hover: hover)").matches) document.addEventListener("mouseout", e => {
  const c = e.target.closest ? e.target.closest("#ppci #panel .cta") : null;
  if (!c) return;
  if (e.relatedTarget ? c.contains(e.relatedTarget) : false) return;
  clearTimeout(STEP_T); clearTimeout(STEP_OUT);
  STEP_OUT = setTimeout(() => showStep(0), 220);
});
if (matchMedia("(hover: hover)").matches) document.addEventListener("mouseover", e => {
  if (e.target.closest ? e.target.closest("#ppci #panel .cta") : null) clearTimeout(STEP_OUT);
  const li = e.target.closest ? e.target.closest("#ppci .steps li.st") : null;
  clearTimeout(STEP_T);
  if (li ? +li.dataset.n !== STEP_ACT : false) STEP_T = setTimeout(() => showStep(+li.dataset.n), 120);
});
function cta(d, name) {
  const CN = `<button type="button" class="cn-link" title="Jump to full details for ${name} below">${name}</button>`;
  const s = signals(d);
  const weak = d.cpi != null ? 35 > d.cpi : false;
  const all = weak ? s.concat(["a low corruption score"]) : s;
  const pep = PP("/politically-exposed-persons-screening/", "Politically Exposed Persons Screening");
  const step = (n, t, x) => `<li class="st" data-n="${n}"><span class="n" aria-hidden="true">${n}</span><div><button type="button" class="st-h" aria-expanded="false">${t.replace(/\.$/, "")}<svg viewBox="0 0 12 12" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M2 4.5l4 4 4-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg></button><div class="st-b">${x}</div></div></li>`;
  const st = [];
  st.push(step(1, "The country.", "Country indicators describe the environment a grant enters: where funds are easier to divert, harder to trace or slower to pay. They say nothing about the grantee itself. " + `The full indicators for ${CN} are in the Country Details box.`));
  st.push(step(2, "The organization.", "The grantee carries risks of its own, separate from the country. Questions include whether it appears on a sanctions or terrorism list under its legal or alternate names, and whether this is a first grant or a large or complex one."));
  st.push(step(3, "The people.", "An organization's name can clear screening while a person who runs the organization is listed. Board members, founders and people with operational authority each carry their own exposure." + " Leaders who hold or have held public office add another."));
  st.push(step(4, "Owners, partners and banks.", "Beneficial owners and controlling entities, subgrantees, partners and vendors, and the banks that move the money all extend a grant beyond the organization. Screening the grantee alone does not reach them."));
  return `<div class="cta"><div class="label">Considering a grant here?</div><p class="cta-in">Country indicators cover one of four areas of grant risk.</p><ol class="steps">${st.join("")}</ol><p class="cta-in cta-foot"><button type="button" class="cta-help">How Paragon helps address these risks <span aria-hidden="true">↓</span></button></p></div>`;
}

function fillDetails() {
  const d = selected ? (info(selected) || {}) : null;
  const key = selected ? selected.properties.name : "";
  const nm = d ? (d.n || key) : "";
  const fc = FLAG[key];
  const flag = fc ? `<img loading="lazy" decoding="async" class="flag" src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/flag-icons@7.2.3/flags/4x3/${fc}.svg" alt="" width="32" height="24" loading="lazy">` : "";
  const cpTile = x => { let v = "", s = "";
    if (x === "cpi") { v = d.cpi == null ? "Not scored" : `${d.cpi}<small> / 100</small>`; s = d.cpi == null ? "Not in the World Bank's governance data." : ""; }
    else if (x === "fatf") { v = d.fatf ? FATF_SHORT[d.fatf] : "Not listed"; s = d.fatf ? FATF_LABEL[d.fatf] + "." : "Not on the FATF black or grey list. Not a finding of low risk."; }
    else if (x === "fto") { if (!d.fto || d.fto === "a") { v = "Not covered"; s = "No chapter in the State Department report."; } else if (d.fto === "n") { v = "None identified"; s = "In the sources reviewed.     else { const J = [["eu", "EU"], ["un", "UN"], ["uk", "UK"], ["ca", "Canada"], ["ch", "Switzerland"], ["au", "Australia"]].filter(j => d[j[0]]).map(j => j[1]); const jl = J.length > 1 ?   const cpSteps = n => { let sp = el("cp-steps"); if (!sp) { const h = el("pp-help"); if (!h || !h.parentNode) return; sp = document.createElement("div"); sp.id = "cp-steps"; h.parentNode.insertBefore(sp, h); } sp.innerHTML = n ? `<p class="cp-eb">Considering a grant here?</p><h3>The country is one part of a grant's risk</h3><p class="cp-in">Country indicators describe the environment a grant enters.   });
  const q = el("q"), b = el("cname"), sh = el("cshare");

    const url = location.origin + location.pathname + "#" + slug(nameOf(selected.properties.name)) + "-" + mode;
    const ok = el("cshare-ok");
    try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url); ok.textContent = "Link copied"; } catch { ok.textContent = url; }
    ok.classList.add("on"); setTimeout(() => ok.classList.remove("on"), 2200);
  });
  if (q) { q.placeholder = selected ? "Search another country" : "Find a country"; if (document.activeElement !== q) q.value = ""; }

  });
}

// Page dates and as-of text from the data file (meta.page); the built-in values above are the fallback.

  }

function findKey(v) {
  v = (v || "").trim().toLowerCase(); if (!v) return null;
  return byName[v] || KEYS.find(k => nameOf(k).toLowerCase().startsWith(v)) || KEYS.find(k => nameOf(k).toLowerCase().includes(v)) || null;
}

// Styled country suggestions, replacing the browser's own dropdown so it matches the page.
function combo(input, onPick) {
  input.removeAttribute("list");
  input.setAttribute("role", "combobox"); input.setAttribute("aria-autocomplete", "list"); input.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
  const w = document.createElement("span"); w.className = "acw"; input.parentNode.insertBefore(w, input); w.appendChild(input);
  const box = document.createElement("div"); box.className = "ac"; box.setAttribute("role", "listbox"); box.hidden = true; w.appendChild(box);
  const names = KEYS.map(nameOf);
  let items = [], idx = -1;
  const close = () => { box.hidden = true; input.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false"); idx = -1; };
  const render = () => { box.innerHTML = items.map((t, i) => `<div role="option" class="${i === idx ? "on" : ""}" data-i="${i}">${t}</div>`).join(""); box.hidden = !items.length; input.setAttribute("aria-expanded", items.length ? "true" : "false"); };
  input.addEventListener("input", () => {
    const s = input.value.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!s) { items = []; close(); return; }
    const starts = names.filter(t => t.toLowerCase().startsWith(s)), within = names.filter(t => !t.toLowerCase().startsWith(s) ? t.toLowerCase().includes(s) : false);
    items = starts.concat(within).slice(0, 8); idx = 0; render();
  });
  input.addEventListener("keydown", e => {
    if (box.hidden) return;
    if (e.key === "ArrowDown") { e.preventDefault(); idx = (idx + 1) % items.length; render(); }
    else if (e.key === "ArrowUp") { e.preventDefault(); idx = (idx - 1 + items.length) % items.length; render(); }
    else if (e.key === "Enter") { if (idx >= 0) input.value = items[idx]; close(); }
    else if (e.key === "Escape") close();
  }, true);
  box.addEventListener("mousedown", e => { const o = e.target.closest("[data-i]"); if (!o) return; e.preventDefault(); input.value = items[+o.dataset.i]; close(); onPick(); });
  input.addEventListener("blur", () => setTimeout(close, 150));
}
combo(q, go);
combo(el("add"), () => el("add-btn").click());

el("all-btn").addEventListener("click", () => { showAll = !showAll; table(); });
el("clear-btn").addEventListener("click", () => { myList = []; save(); table(); if (selected) render(); });

const COLS = [["name", "Country"], ["cpi", "Corruption control"], ["fatf", "FATF"], ["fto", "FTO presence"], ["us", "U.S. sanctions"], ["eu", "EU measures"], ["un", "UN regimes"]];
function rank(d, c) {
  d = d || {};
  if (c === "cpi") return d.cpi == null ? 999 : d.cpi;               // lower score sorts first: more to consider
  if (c === "fatf") return d.fatf === "black" ? 0 : d.fatf === "grey" ? 1 : 2;
  if (c === "fto") return d.fto === "y" ? 0 : d.fto === "n" ? 2 : 1;
  if (c === "us") return d.us === "c" ? 0 : d.us === "t" ? 1 : 2;
  if (c === "eu") return d.eu ? 0 : 1;
  if (c === "un") return d.un ? 0 : 1;
  return 0;
}
function cell(d, c) {
  d = d || {};
  const st = { cpi: "cpi", fatf: "fatf", fto: "fto", us: "sanc", eu: "sanc", un: "sanc" }[c];
  if (isStale(st)) return "Being updated";
  if (c === "cpi") return d.cpi != null ? `${sw(cpiColor(d.cpi))}${d.cpi}` : "Not scored";
  if (c === "fatf") return d.fatf ? sw(d.fatf === "black" ? css("--call") : css("--grey-list")) + FATF_SHORT[d.fatf] : "Not listed";
  if (c === "fto") return d.fto === "y" ? `${sw(css("--fto"))}Identified (${d.ftog.length})` : d.fto === "n" ? "None identified" : "Not covered";
  if (c === "us") return d.us === "c" ? `${sw(css("--embargo"))}Comprehensive` : d.us === "t" ? "Targeted" : "None";
  if (c === "eu") return d.eu ? `${d.eu.length} in force` : "None";
  if (c === "un") return d.un ? `${d.un.length} regime${d.un.length > 1 ? "s" : ""}` : "None";
}
function table() {
  el("all-btn").textContent = showAll ? "Show only my list" : "Show all countries";
  el("chips").innerHTML = myList.map(k => `<span class="chip">${nameOf(k)}<button type="button" aria-label="Remove ${nameOf(k)}" data-k="${k}">×</button></span>`).join("");
  el("chips").querySelectorAll("button").forEach(b => b.addEventListener("click", () => removeCountry(b.dataset.k)));
  el("thead").innerHTML = COLS.map(([c, t]) => `<th scope="col" aria-sort="${sortCol === c ? (sortDir > 0 ? "ascending" : "descending") : "none"}"><button type="button" data-c="${c}">${t}${sortCol === c ? (sortDir > 0 ? " ↑" : " ↓") : ""}</button></th>`).join("");
  el("thead").querySelectorAll("button").forEach(b => b.addEventListener("click", () => { const c = b.dataset.c; sortDir = sortCol === c ? -sortDir : 1; sortCol = c; table(); }));
  let rows = showAll ? KEYS.slice() : myList.slice();
  rows.sort((a, b) => sortCol === "name" ? sortDir * nameOf(a).localeCompare(nameOf(b)) : sortDir * (rank(RISK[a], sortCol) - rank(RISK[b], sortCol)) || nameOf(a).localeCompare(nameOf(b)));
  el("tbody").innerHTML = rows.length ? rows.map(k => `<tr class="row" data-k="${k}"><th scope="row" style="position:static;font:500 13.5px var(--body);text-transform:none;letter-spacing:0;color:var(--fg)">${nameOf(k)}</th>${COLS.slice(1).map(([c]) => `<td class="${c === "cpi" ? "num" : ""}">${cell(RISK[k], c)}</td>`).join("")}</tr>`).join("")
    : `<tr><td colspan="7" class="sub">No countries yet. Add the countries your grantees work in, or show all countries.</td></tr>`;
  el("tbody").querySelectorAll("tr.row").forEach(r => r.addEventListener("click", () => { select(byKey[r.dataset.k], true); el("globe").scrollIntoView({ behavior: reduce ? "auto" : "smooth", block: "center" }); }));
}

// Links to a country and view, e.g. #kenya-sanc
function writeHash() {
  const h = selected ? slug(nameOf(selected.properties.name)) + "-" + mode : "";
  try { const cur = decodeURIComponent((location.hash || "").slice(1)); if (cur !== h) history.pushState(null, "", h ? "#" + h : location.pathname + location.search); } catch {}
}
function readHash() {
  const h = decodeURIComponent((location.hash || "").slice(1)); if (!h) return;
  const m = MODES.find(x => h.endsWith("-" + x)), base = m ? h.slice(0, -(m.length + 1)) : h;
  if (m) setMode(m);
  if (bySlug[base]) select(byKey[bySlug[base]], true);
}
function onNav() { if (!location.hash) { if (selected) select(null); return; } readHash(); }
addEventListener("popstate", onNav);
addEventListener("hashchange", onNav);
matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)").addEventListener("change", () => { world.polygonCapColor(capFill).pointsData(dots()); legend(); render(); table(); });
legend(); render(); table(); readHash();

})();

  window.addEventListener("resize", fit);
})();

/* Country profile design polish, 2026-11-24 (SE-85). Keep this script free of double ampersands and less-than signs: WordPress texturize can rewrite them. */
(function(){
  function polish(){
    var box = document.getElementById("cdet-box"); if (!box) return;
    box.querySelectorAll(".cp-k").forEach(function(k){
      if (k.querySelector(".pp-lc")) return;
      if (k.textContent !== "U.S.-designated FTOs") return;
      k.textContent = "U.S.-designated FTO";
      var sp = document.createElement("span"); sp.className = "pp-lc"; sp.textContent = "s"; k.appendChild(sp);
    });
    box.querySelectorAll(".sjd").forEach(function(d){
      if (d.getAttribute("data-pp") === "1") return;
      d.setAttribute("data-pp", "1");
      Array.prototype.slice.call(d.childNodes).forEach(function(n){ if (n.nodeType === 3) { n.nodeValue = n.nodeValue.replace(/^[\s.;,]+/, ""); if (n.nodeValue === "") n.parentNode.removeChild(n); } });
      d.querySelectorAll("a").forEach(function(a){
        var t = a.lastChild; if (!t) return; if (t.nodeType !== 3) return;
        var s = t.nodeValue, i = s.lastIndexOf(" ");
        var w = document.createElement("span"); w.className = "pp-lw"; w.textContent = s.slice(i + 1);
        t.nodeValue = s.slice(0, i + 1); a.appendChild(w); a.classList.add("pp-af");
      });
    });
  }
  function start(){
    var box = document.getElementById("cdet-box");
    if (!box) { setTimeout(start, 300); return; }
    polish();
    new MutationObserver(polish).observe(box, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  }
  start();
})();

/* Phone-only behaviour, 2026-11-24 (SE-85). Every action is gated on max-width 767px, so desktop is unaffected. */
(function(){
  var mq = window.matchMedia("(max-width: 767px)");
  var beh = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches ? "auto" : "smooth";
  var last = null, booted = false;
  function name(p){ var h = p.querySelector("h2"); if (!h) return null; var t = h.textContent.trim(); return t === "Select a country" ? "" : t; }
  function fixEmpty(p){ if (!mq.matches) return; var em = p.querySelector(".empty"); if (em) if (em.textContent.indexOf("Search above") !== 0) em.textContent = "Search above, or tap a country on the globe."; }
  function toResult(p){
    var a = document.activeElement; if (a) { if (a.id === "q") a.blur(); }
    var go = function(){ var t = document.getElementById("cdet-box") || p; t.scrollIntoView({ behavior: beh, block: "start" }); return t; };
    var t = go();
    setTimeout(function(){ var y = t.getBoundingClientRect().top; if (y > 80 || -20 > y) go(); }, 900);
  }
  function watch(){
    var p = document.getElementById("panel");
    if (!p) { setTimeout(watch, 300); return; }
    last = name(p); fixEmpty(p);
    setTimeout(function(){ booted = true; var n = name(p); if (n !== null) last = n; }, 1500);
    new MutationObserver(function(){
      fixEmpty(p);
      var n = name(p);
      if (n === null) return;
      if (n) if (n !== last) if (booted) if (mq.matches) setTimeout(function(){ toResult(p); }, 250);
      last = n;
    }).observe(p, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  }
  /* Keep the indicator tiles still when switching indicators: the map key above changes height per indicator. */
  var pin = null;
  function boxTop(){ var b = document.getElementById("cdet-box"); return b ? b.getBoundingClientRect().top : null; }
  function hold(){
    if (pin === null) return;
    var t = boxTop(); if (t === null) return;
    var d = t - pin; if (Math.abs(d) > 0.5) {
      var h = document.documentElement, sb = h.style.scrollBehavior;
      h.style.scrollBehavior = "auto"; window.scrollTo(0, window.scrollY + d); h.style.scrollBehavior = sb;
    }
  }
  document.addEventListener("click", function(e){
    if (!mq.matches) return;
    var c = e.target.closest ? e.target.closest("#cdet-box .cp-t") : null;
    if (!c) return;
    pin = boxTop();
    var end = performance.now() + 700;
    (function loop(){ hold(); if (performance.now() > end) { pin = null; return; } requestAnimationFrame(loop); })();
  }, true);
  window.addEventListener("click", function(){ hold(); });
  watch();
})();

				const lazyloadRunObserver = () => {
					const lazyloadBackgrounds = document.querySelectorAll( `.e-con.e-parent:not(.e-lazyloaded)` );
					const lazyloadBackgroundObserver = new IntersectionObserver( ( entries ) => {
						entries.forEach( ( entry ) => {
							if ( entry.isIntersecting ) {
								let lazyloadBackground = entry.target;
								if( lazyloadBackground ) {
									lazyloadBackground.classList.add( 'e-lazyloaded' );
								}
								lazyloadBackgroundObserver.unobserve( entry.target );
							}
						});
					}, { rootMargin: '200px 0px 200px 0px' } );
					lazyloadBackgrounds.forEach( ( lazyloadBackground ) => {
						lazyloadBackgroundObserver.observe( lazyloadBackground );
					} );
				};
				const events = [
					'DOMContentLoaded',
					'elementor/lazyload/observe',
				];
				events.forEach( ( event ) => {
					document.addEventListener( event, lazyloadRunObserver );

/* pp-toc-follow (2026-10-14): on desktop, a copy of the "On this page" box follows the reader in the right margin,
   marks the current section, and steps aside wherever page content would sit underneath it. */
(function () {
  function init() {
    var orig = document.querySelector('.elementor-widget.pp-toc:not(.pp-toc-float)');
    if (!orig || document.querySelector('.pp-toc-float')) return;
    var fl = orig.cloneNode(true);
    fl.classList.add('pp-toc-float');
    fl.removeAttribute('data-id');
    fl.removeAttribute('id');
    fl.setAttribute('role', 'navigation');
    fl.setAttribute('aria-label', 'On this page');
    document.body.appendChild(fl);

    var bar = document.getElementById('wpadminbar');
    var targets = [];
    orig.querySelectorAll('a[href^="#"]').forEach(function (a) {
      var el = document.getElementById(a.getAttribute('href').slice(1));
      if (el) targets.push({ id: a.getAttribute('href').slice(1), el: el });
    });
    var links = [].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('.pp-toc a[href^="#"]'));
    var ticking = false;

    function topOffset() { return 24 + (bar ? bar.offsetHeight : 0); }

    function blocked(x0, y0, w, h) {
      var xs = [x0 + 6, x0 + w / 2, x0 + w - 6];
      var ys = [y0 + 4, y0 + h / 3, y0 + (2 * h) / 3, y0 + h - 4];
      for (var i = 0; i < xs.length; i++) {
        for (var j = 0; j < ys.length; j++) {
          var els = document.elementsFromPoint(xs[i], ys[j]);
          for (var k = 0; k < els.length; k++) {
            var el = els[k];
            if (el === orig || orig.contains(el)) continue;
            if (el.closest('footer, .site-footer, #colophon')) return true;
            var wdg = el.closest('.elementor-widget');
            if (wdg && (wdg.matches('.pp-related, .pp-rel-w, .pp-allsvc-w, .pp-stats, .pp-cl-map') || wdg.closest('.pp-sp-side, .pp-clients-v2') || wdg.querySelector('.pp-clients-v2'))) continue; /* 2026-10-16: never step aside for side-column cards */
            if (wdg && !wdg.classList.contains('elementor-widget-spacer') && wdg.offsetHeight > 0) return true;
          }
        }
      }
      return false;
    }

    var marks = [];
    [orig, fl].forEach(function (w) {
      var box = w.querySelector('.elementor-widget-container') || w;
      var mk = document.createElement('span'); mk.className = 'pp-toc-mk'; mk.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
      var fill = document.createElement('i'); mk.appendChild(fill); box.appendChild(mk);
      marks.push({ box: box, mk: mk, fill: fill });
    });

    var endLatch = false; /* 2026-11-13 */
    function update() {
      ticking = false;
      if (window.innerWidth < 1025) { fl.classList.remove('is-on'); return; }
      var top = topOffset();
      fl.style.setProperty('--pp-float-top', top + 'px');
      var r = orig.getBoundingClientRect();
      fl.style.left = r.left + 'px';
      fl.style.width = r.width + 'px';
      var show = r.top < top; /* 2026-10-16: take over as soon as the box starts to scroll under the header */
      if (show) {
        fl.classList.add('pp-probe');
        var h = Math.min(fl.offsetHeight, window.innerHeight - top - 24);
        show = !blocked(r.left, top, r.width, h);
        fl.classList.remove('pp-probe');
      }
      var cur = null;
      for (var i = 0; i < targets.length; i++) {
        if (targets[i].el.getBoundingClientRect().top <= top + 140) cur = targets[i].id;
      }
      /* 2026-11-13: once the reader is in the last listed section and the box first steps aside, it stays hidden until they scroll back up (no flicker over full-width bands below) */
      var lastId = targets.length ? targets[targets.length - 1].id : null;
      if (cur !== lastId) endLatch = false;
      else if (r.top < top && !show) endLatch = true;
      if (endLatch) show = false;
      fl.classList.toggle('is-on', show);
      links.forEach(function (a) { a.classList.toggle('is-active', a.getAttribute('href') === '#' + cur); });
      /* 2026-10-16 pp-toc-mk: sliding marker with section progress */
      var prog = 0, ci = -1;
      for (var j = 0; j < targets.length; j++) { if (targets[j].id === cur) ci = j; }
      if (ci > -1) {
        var s0 = targets[ci].el.getBoundingClientRect().top;
        var main = document.querySelector('.pp-sp-main');
        var s1 = ci + 1 < targets.length ? targets[ci + 1].el.getBoundingClientRect().top : (main ? main.getBoundingClientRect().bottom : s0 + 800);
        prog = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (top + 140 - s0) / Math.max(1, s1 - s0)));
      }
      marks.forEach(function (m) {
        var act = m.box.querySelector('a.is-active');
        if (!act) { m.mk.classList.remove('on'); return; }
        var cr = m.box.getBoundingClientRect(), ar = act.getBoundingClientRect();
        m.mk.style.top = (ar.top - cr.top + m.box.scrollTop) + 'px';
        m.mk.style.height = ar.height + 'px';
        m.mk.style.left = (ar.left - cr.left - 12) + 'px';
        m.fill.style.height = Math.round(prog * 100) + '%';
        m.mk.classList.add('on');
      });
    }

    function onScroll() {
      if (ticking) return;
      ticking = true;
      window.requestAnimationFrame(update);
      setTimeout(function () { if (ticking) update(); }, 120);
    }
    window.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('resize', onScroll);
    window.addEventListener('load', update);
    update();
  }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init); else init();
})();

/* pp-a11y-selects (2026-10-16): newsletter form dropdowns get their visible label as an accessible name */
(function () {
  function fix() {
    document.querySelectorAll('form.emailoctopus-form select:not([aria-label])').forEach(function (s) {
      var row = s.closest('.emailoctopus-form-row, .form-group') || s.parentElement;
      var lab = row && row.querySelector('label');
      if (lab && lab.textContent.trim()) s.setAttribute('aria-label', lab.textContent.trim());
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('form.emailoctopus-form [role="button"][tabindex="-1"]').forEach(function (b) { b.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true'); });
  }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', fix); else fix();
  window.addEventListener('load', fix);
})();
